<a id="writing-history"></a>
## 历史作文记录

<a id="material-001"></a>
### 001 城市生活与自然

**材料类型：**英语六级写作　　**整理日期：**2026-10-09　　**原题来源：**2026年6月大学英语六级考试真题（第1套）

**材料说明：**个人英文作文此前已提供；2026-10-18根据用户上传的试卷图片补录2026年6月大学英语六级考试真题（第1套）的完整写作要求。下面保留个人原文，再给出便于学习的中文理解和轻度润色版，不推断考试得分。

#### 作文题目（图片转录）

> **Directions:** For this part, you are allowed 30 minutes to write an essay that begins with the sentence “With the trend of urbanization, it is vitally important for city residents to develop a stronger love for nature.” You can make comments, cite examples or use your personal experiences to develop your essay. You should write at least 150 words but no more than 200 words.
>
> **You should copy the sentence given in quotes at the beginning of your essay.**

**要求提要：**30分钟；150—200词；开头必须逐字抄写引号中的指定句。下方个人原文使用了指定开头句。

#### 用户提供的英文原文

With the trend of urbanization, it is vitally important for city residents to develop a stronger love for nature. As cities grow, many people spend more time indoors and have fewer chances to enjoy the natural world. This makes it necessary to bring nature back into our daily lives.

First, spending time in nature can help people reduce stress. For example, taking a walk in a park after work allows them to relax and forget their worries for a while. Second, a love for nature can encourage residents to protect the environment. People who value green spaces are more likely to save water, reduce waste and take part in activities such as planting trees. These small actions can make cities better places to live.

Therefore, both individuals and local governments should take action. Residents can spend more time outdoors and learn about plants and animals. Meanwhile, governments can build more parks and organize environmental activities. By working together, we can enjoy healthier lives and create greener cities.

#### 中文理解（根据英文整理，不是另行提供的中文原题）

随着城市化的发展，城市居民培养更强的自然热爱之情十分重要。随着城市扩张，许多人在室内待的时间更长，享受自然的机会更少。因此，有必要把自然重新带回日常生活。

首先，亲近自然可以帮助人们减轻压力。例如，下班后在公园散步能让人放松，暂时忘记烦恼。其次，热爱自然可以鼓励居民保护环境。珍视绿地的人更可能节约用水、减少浪费，并参加植树等活动。这些小小的行动可以让城市变得更加宜居。

因此，个人和地方政府都应采取行动。居民可以多在户外活动，了解植物和动物。与此同时，政府可以建设更多公园并组织环保活动。通过共同努力，我们能够享有更健康的生活，并建设更绿色的城市。

#### 学习用润色版

As urbanization continues, it is important for city residents to develop a greater appreciation for nature. As cities grow, many people spend more time indoors and have fewer chances to enjoy the natural world. This makes it necessary to bring nature back into our daily lives.

First, spending time in nature can help people reduce stress. For example, taking a walk in a park after work allows them to relax and forget their worries for a while. Second, a love for nature can encourage residents to protect the environment. People who value green spaces are more likely to save water, reduce waste and take part in activities such as planting trees. These small actions can make cities better places to live.

Therefore, both individuals and local governments should take action. Residents can spend more time outdoors and learn about plants and animals. Meanwhile, governments can build more parks and organize environmental activities. By working together, we can enjoy healthier lives and create greener cities.

**修改说明：**原文整体语法和结构清楚。本版主要将开头改为更自然的 `As urbanization continues`，并使用 `develop a greater appreciation for nature` 表达“更加欣赏、重视自然”。但真题明确要求作文以指定句开头，因此该润色版只用于比较表达，不能直接作为本题考场版本；正式作答必须保留原题指定开头。原来的 `develop a stronger love for nature` 可以理解；初学时先记 `develop a love for...` 即可。`vitally important` 语法正确，只是语气更强。不把润色选择当作唯一正确答案。

<a id="writing-outline-001"></a>
#### 写作结构与可迁移思路

| 段落 | 本篇功能 | 可以模仿的做法 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第一段 | 城市发展背景 → 接触自然的机会减少 → 提出观点 | 先写一个具体变化，再说明为什么需要行动，不只写空泛的“非常重要” |
| 第二段 | 减轻压力 + 保护环境，两点理由 | 每个理由配一个例子或具体行为，如散步、节水、植树 |
| 第三段 | 个人与政府分别行动 → 合作结果 | 说清“谁做什么”；建议应能对应前文问题 |

本篇的 `First / Second / For example / Therefore / Meanwhile` 各有作用。模仿时保留逻辑，不必把所有连接词都机械塞入每一篇作文。

#### 本篇优先背诵清单（12 项）

1. it is important for somebody to do something（对某人来说，做某事很重要）
2. develop a love for...（培养对……的热爱）
3. spend time in nature（在自然中度过时间／亲近自然）
4. have fewer chances to do something（有更少的机会做某事）
5. reduce stress（减轻压力）
6. allow somebody to do something（允许／使某人有条件做某事）
7. encourage somebody to do something（鼓励／促使某人做某事）
8. protect the environment（保护环境）
9. be more likely to do something（更有可能做某事）
10. take part in...（参加……）
11. take action to do something（采取行动做某事）
12. by working together（通过共同努力）

先背这12项；`urbanization`、`appreciation` 等拓展词可稍后复习。旧材料中的 `help somebody do / to do something` 与 `make it + adjective + to do something` 在本篇继续复现，已在总表合并来源。

<a id="writing-notes-001"></a>
#### 关键语法与易错点

1. **`it is important for somebody to do something`。** `it` 是形式主语，后面的不定式说明具体事项：`It is important for residents to protect nature.` 练习本句型时不要漏掉 `for` 或 `to`，不能写 `It is important for residents protect nature.` 原文 `vitally important` 中的 `vitally` 是副词，修饰形容词 `important`；不能改成 `vital important`。
2. **`develop` 的新用法。** 在翻译001中可表示发展、制定；本篇 `develop a love for nature` 指逐渐形成或培养情感，不是“开发自然”。`development` 是名词，不能代替动词：`We can develop a love for reading.`
3. **`spend` 后面的结构。** `spend time indoors / outdoors` 用地点副词；`spend time in nature` 用介词短语；花时间做事用 `spend time doing something`，不用 `spend time to do something`。`outdoors` 是副词，`outdoor` 是形容词：`spend time outdoors` / `outdoor activities`。
4. **`fewer` 与 `less`。** `chances` 是复数可数名词，因此用 `fewer chances`；`time` 在此不可数，因此用 `less time`。不写 `less chances`。
5. **`make it necessary to do something`。** 与翻译001的 `make it easier for somebody to do something` 共用 `make + it + 形容词 + (for + 人) + to do` 结构；`for + 人` 可按需要省略。`This makes it necessary to bring nature back into our daily lives.` 中 `it` 是形式宾语；`This` 作主语，谓语用 `makes`。
6. **`help / allow / encourage / enable` 的区别。** `help somebody do / to do` 两种都可用；`allow / encourage / enable somebody to do` 在这些句型中都保留 `to`。它们分别侧重帮助、许可或提供条件、鼓励、使能够，不能因为中文接近就任意互换。
7. **动名词短语作主语。** `Spending time in nature helps people relax.` 和 `Taking a walk allows them to relax.` 中，整个动作短语通常看作单数，分别用 `helps`、`allows`。原文 `spending time ... can help` 中，情态动词 `can` 后用动词原形。
8. **`be more likely to do` 表示可能性。** `People who value green spaces are more likely to protect them.` 中 `people` 是复数，关系从句用 `value`，主句用 `are`；该表达不等于“必然会”。
9. **并列与举例。** 原文 `save water, reduce waste and take part in...` 三个动词形式并列；`such as planting trees` 中用动名词。对比：`outdoor activities such as walking` / `For example, residents can walk in a park.`
10. **`by` 后接动名词。** `By working together, we can create greener cities.` 不用 `by work` 或 `by to work`；共同努力的人是后面的 `we`。换主语时也要保证逻辑一致。
11. **让某地变得宜居。** `make cities better places to live` 是 `make + 宾语 + 名词补足语`：复数 `cities` 对应 `places`；单数可写 `make our city a better place to live`。
12. **词形与连接词。** `environment` 是名词，`environmental` 是形容词；用 `protect the environment`、`environmental activities`。`Therefore` 表示因果，`Meanwhile` 表示同时或并行，不合并成同一种逻辑连接词。

#### 迁移练习（先做题，再展开答案）

1. 阅读活动可以鼓励学生培养对阅读的热爱，并帮助他们减轻压力。
2. 忙碌的城市居民有更少的机会锻炼，因此有必要让他们更容易参加户外活动。
3. 个人和地方政府应共同采取行动，节约用水、减少浪费，使城市变得更加宜居。

<details>
<summary>参考答案</summary>

1. Reading activities can encourage students to develop a love for reading and help them reduce stress.
2. Busy city residents have fewer chances to exercise. Therefore, it is necessary to make it easier for them to take part in outdoor activities.
3. Individuals and local governments should take action together to save water, reduce waste and make cities better places to live.

参考答案不唯一。复盘时检查 `encourage + 人 + to do`、`fewer chances`、`make it easier for + 人 + to do`，以及并列动词形式是否一致。

</details>

**复习标记（用户填写）：**□ 未背　□ 初背　□ 需复习　□ 已能迁移造句  
**个人备注（用户填写）：**

<a id="material-003"></a>
### 003 传统文化与文化自信

**材料类型：**写作　　**题目信息：**2026年6月英语六级写作第3套（按用户提供文本整理）　　**整理日期：**2026-10-18

#### 题目要求与中文理解

> **Directions:** For this part, you are allowed 30 minutes to write an essay that begins with the sentence: “Faced with growing globalization, students realize that a deep understanding of traditional culture helps cultivate cultural confidence.” You can make comments, cite examples or use your experiences to develop your essay. You should write at least 150 words but no more than 200 words.

**中文理解：**面对日益发展的全球化，学生们意识到，深入理解传统文化有助于培养文化自信。

**作答要求：**30分钟；150—200词；必须在作文开头原样使用指定英文句子，可发表评论、举例或结合个人经历。指定句应计入全文词数。

#### 三段式写作结构（学习指导，不是个人作文）

1. **引出主题：**先原样写指定开头句，再表明观点。例如：In my opinion, traditional culture plays an important role in helping young people develop a strong sense of cultural identity.
2. **分析原因：**其一，学习传统文化有助于建立文化认同：First of all, learning about traditional culture enables students to develop a stronger sense of cultural identity. 其二，了解传统文化有助于跨文化交流：Furthermore, a deep understanding of traditional culture allows students to introduce Chinese culture to people from different countries. 可用传统节日举例：For example, students can introduce traditional Chinese festivals, such as the Spring Festival and the Mid-Autumn Festival, to their foreign friends.
3. **总结建议：**回扣观点并提出可行行动。例如：In conclusion, traditional culture is essential to developing cultural confidence. Therefore, students should actively learn about Chinese traditions and share them with the world.

这些句子是可选择的示范素材，写作时应根据论证需要取舍，不能把它们拼接成一篇已完成作文。

#### 核心词汇与搭配（20项）

下表是本题学习视图；与旧词条重合的表达只在此补充本题用法，不再作为独立的总表新词条。`enable somebody to do something` 已见[翻译积累的共用表达](./翻译积累.md#translation-patterns)。

| 英文表达 | 中文释义 | 用法 | 可复用例句 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| globalization | 全球化 | 不可数名词；`growing globalization` 表示全球化发展。 | Globalization creates new opportunities for cultural exchange. |
| traditional culture | 传统文化 | `traditional` 为形容词，修饰 `culture`。 | Schools can help students learn about traditional culture. |
| cultural confidence | 文化自信 | `cultural` 为形容词；常与 `develop`、`cultivate` 连用。 | Learning local history can strengthen cultural confidence. |
| cultural identity | 文化认同 | 常见搭配 `a sense of cultural identity`。 | Family stories can give children a sense of cultural identity. |
| cultivate | 培养 | 可接 `confidence`、`habits` 等名词。 | Reading can cultivate curiosity in young people. |
| strengthen | 增强 | 可接 `confidence`、`cooperation` 等名词。 | Team projects can strengthen students' communication skills. |
| enable somebody to do something | 使某人能够做某事 | `enable + 宾语 + to + 动词原形`；复用已有跨册表达。 | Digital libraries enable students to access books from home. |
| play an important role in | 在……中发挥重要作用 | `in` 后接名词或动名词。 | Exercise plays an important role in maintaining good health. |
| a deep understanding of | 对……的深入理解 | `understanding` 为名词，后接 `of + 对象`。 | A deep understanding of local needs helps improve public services. |
| introduce A to B | 向B介绍A | `A` 是介绍的事物，`B` 是接受介绍的人。 | Volunteers introduce local history to visitors. |
| cultural exchange | 文化交流 | 可说 `promote cultural exchange`。 | Travel can promote cultural exchange. |
| traditional Chinese festivals | 中国传统节日 | 具体节日举例时可用 `such as`。 | Schools can organize events about traditional Chinese festivals. |
| the Spring Festival | 春节 | 节日名称首字母大写，前用 `the`。 | Many families gather during the Spring Festival. |
| the Mid-Autumn Festival | 中秋节 | 节日名称首字母大写，前用 `the`。 | People often share mooncakes during the Mid-Autumn Festival. |
| be essential to doing something | 对做某事至关重要 | 这里的 `to` 是介词，后接动名词。 | Clean water is essential to protecting public health. |
| develop a sense of identity | 建立认同感 | `develop + a sense of + 名词`。 | Community activities help children develop a sense of identity. |
| broaden one's horizons | 拓宽视野 | `one's` 按主语变化；`horizons` 通常用复数。 | Reading widely can broaden students' horizons. |
| preserve traditional culture | 保护传统文化 | `preserve` 强调保护并传承。 | Museums help preserve traditional culture. |
| promote cultural understanding | 促进文化理解 | `promote + 名词短语`。 | Student exchanges can promote cultural understanding. |
| share Chinese culture with the world | 向世界传播中国文化 | `share A with B`，介词用 `with`。 | Young artists can share Chinese culture with the world. |

#### 可复用写作句型（8项）

下表是本题的句型应用视图。`Therefore` 和 `enable...to do...` 在旧资料中已有基础用法，此处仅补充新场景；句型可迁移，但不能机械拼接。

| 英文模板 | 中文意思 | 使用场景 | 原创例句 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| In my opinion, ... plays an important role in ... | 我认为……在……中发挥重要作用 | 提出观点 | In my opinion, regular exercise plays an important role in maintaining good health. |
| One important reason is that ... | 一个重要原因是…… | 分析原因 | One important reason is that online courses give rural students more learning opportunities. |
| ... enables somebody to do something. | ……使某人能够做某事 | 说明作用 | Digital tools enable farmers to reach more customers. |
| Furthermore, ... | 此外，…… | 补充论点 | Furthermore, public transport can reduce traffic congestion. |
| For example, ... | 例如，…… | 举例支撑论点 | For example, schools can organize recycling activities. |
| In conclusion, ... | 总之，…… | 收束观点 | In conclusion, reading helps students think more independently. |
| Therefore, students should ... | 因此，学生应当…… | 根据前文提出建议 | Therefore, students should learn to check sources before sharing information. |
| ... is essential to doing something. | ……对做某事至关重要 | 强调必要条件 | Adequate sleep is essential to maintaining good health. |

#### 写作注意事项

1. 指定开头句必须原样使用；全文控制在150—200词，写作时间30分钟。
2. 优先保证切题、三段结构清楚和逻辑完整，避免为了“高级表达”牺牲语法准确性。
3. 检查主谓一致、时态、冠词和介词；举例应支持论点，避免重复表述相同观点。
4. `be essential to doing something` 中的 `to` 是介词，后接动名词：Traditional culture is essential to developing cultural confidence.

#### 个人学习记录（2026-10-18）

**学习内容：**2026年6月英语六级写作第3套“传统文化与文化自信”。按本次提供的信息，题目理解、三段式结构、核心词汇和可复用句型已整理。

**当前进度：**独立作文尚未完成；尚未进行作文评分。本章没有个人原始作文、写作错误或分数记录。

**后续可补充：**个人原始作文、修改稿、基于实际作文的语法问题与词汇替换，以及有依据的评分和改进建议。

<a id="material-004"></a>
### 004｜2024年6月第1套｜数字素养与数字技能

**材料类型：**六级写作议论文　　**英文主题：**Digital Literacy and Skills　　**学习日期：**2026-10-23　　**题目来源：**按本次提供的题目文本整理。

#### 真题信息与题目要求

> **Directions:** For this part, you are allowed 30 minutes to write an essay that begins with the sentence: “There is a growing awareness of the importance of digital literacy and skills in today's world.” You can make comments, cite examples or use your personal experiences to develop your essay. You should write at least 150 words but no more than 200 words (not including the sentence given).

**作答要求：**指定开头句须原样放在正文开头；除该句外正文150—200词；写作时间30分钟。本章仅收录学习参考范文，没有个人原始作文或官方评分。

#### 学习参考范文

**The Importance of Digital Literacy and Skills**

There is a growing awareness of the importance of digital literacy and skills in today's world. In my opinion, these abilities are essential for students to study effectively and prepare for their future careers.

First of all, digital skills help students obtain information and learn more efficiently. For example, they can use online libraries to find useful materials and attend courses offered by universities around the world. However, students should also learn to judge whether online information is reliable. Otherwise, they may be misled by false or inaccurate content. Furthermore, digital literacy plays an important role in the workplace. As technology develops rapidly, many jobs require employees to use digital tools to communicate, analyze information, and solve problems. Students who master these skills will be better prepared for future challenges. In addition, protecting personal information is another important part of digital literacy. By understanding online risks, students can use the Internet more safely and responsibly.

In conclusion, digital literacy is not only a useful skill but also a necessary ability in modern society. Therefore, universities should provide more practical training, while students should actively improve their digital skills. Only in this way can young people make better use of technology and achieve personal development.

**程序字数统计：**指定开头句 16 词；其余正文 188 词；全文 204 词。按题目规定排除开头句后，正文符合150—200词要求。词数用英文字母、撇号及连字符识别英文单词，标点不单独计词。

#### 中英文逐句对照

1. **英文：**There is a growing awareness of the importance of digital literacy and skills in today's world.<br>
   **中文：**如今，人们越来越意识到数字素养和数字技能的重要性。

2. **英文：**In my opinion, these abilities are essential for students to study effectively and prepare for their future careers.<br>
   **中文：**我认为，这些能力对学生高效学习和为未来职业生涯做准备至关重要。

3. **英文：**First of all, digital skills help students obtain information and learn more efficiently.<br>
   **中文：**首先，数字技能帮助学生获取信息并更高效地学习。

4. **英文：**For example, they can use online libraries to find useful materials and attend courses offered by universities around the world.<br>
   **中文：**例如，他们可以使用在线图书馆查找有用资料，并参加世界各地大学开设的课程。

5. **英文：**However, students should also learn to judge whether online information is reliable.<br>
   **中文：**然而，学生也应学会判断网上信息是否可靠。

6. **英文：**Otherwise, they may be misled by false or inaccurate content.<br>
   **中文：**否则，他们可能被虚假或不准确的内容误导。

7. **英文：**Furthermore, digital literacy plays an important role in the workplace.<br>
   **中文：**此外，数字素养在职场中发挥重要作用。

8. **英文：**As technology develops rapidly, many jobs require employees to use digital tools to communicate, analyze information, and solve problems.<br>
   **中文：**随着技术快速发展，许多岗位要求员工使用数字工具进行沟通、分析信息和解决问题。

9. **英文：**Students who master these skills will be better prepared for future challenges.<br>
   **中文：**掌握这些技能的学生将能更好地迎接未来挑战。

10. **英文：**In addition, protecting personal information is another important part of digital literacy.<br>
   **中文：**此外，保护个人信息也是数字素养的另一个重要方面。

11. **英文：**By understanding online risks, students can use the Internet more safely and responsibly.<br>
   **中文：**了解网络风险有助于学生更安全、更负责任地使用互联网。

12. **英文：**In conclusion, digital literacy is not only a useful skill but also a necessary ability in modern society.<br>
   **中文：**总之，数字素养不仅是一项实用技能，也是现代社会的一项必备能力。

13. **英文：**Therefore, universities should provide more practical training, while students should actively improve their digital skills.<br>
   **中文：**因此，大学应提供更多实践培训，学生也应积极提升数字技能。

14. **英文：**Only in this way can young people make better use of technology and achieve personal development.<br>
   **中文：**只有这样，年轻人才能更好地利用技术，实现个人发展。

#### 三段式结构分析

1. **第一段：引出主题。**保留指定开头句，随后用 `In my opinion, ... are essential for ...` 提出观点，连接学习效率与职业准备两个方向。
2. **第二段：分析原因。**先写数字技能帮助获取信息与高效学习，再以在线图书馆和大学课程举例，并补充信息可靠性；接着写职场需求；最后写个人信息保护和网络风险。例子均服务于相应论点。
3. **第三段：总结建议。**概括数字素养的双重作用，建议大学提供实践培训、学生主动提升技能，以 `Only in this way can ...` 的部分倒装收束。

<a id="writing-vocabulary-004"></a>
#### 核心词汇与搭配（20项）

本表是写作004的主题学习视图。`play an important role in` 复用[写作003](#material-003)的已有词条；`protect personal information` 作为旧词条 `protect / protection` 的新搭配，不另建同义总表条目。其余表达按本题语境整理，以下例句只用于模仿，不代表个人写作成果。

| 英语表达 | 中文释义 | 词性或固定搭配 | 可复用英文例句 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| digital literacy | 数字素养 | 名词短语；literacy 在此指理解和使用数字信息的能力 | Digital literacy helps people use online services wisely. |
| digital skills | 数字技能 | 可数复数名词短语；具体操作技能 | Digital skills can help workers complete tasks efficiently. |
| growing awareness | 日益增强的意识 | 名词短语；常接 of + 事物 | There is growing awareness of the need to save energy. |
| be essential for | 对……至关重要 | be + 形容词 + for + 名词或动名词；与 essential to doing 的结构不同 | Regular exercise is essential for good health. |
| prepare for future careers | 为未来职业做准备 | 动词短语；prepare for + 名词 | Internships help students prepare for future careers. |
| obtain information | 获取信息 | 动词短语；obtain 后直接接宾语 | Students can obtain information from public libraries. |
| learn efficiently | 高效学习 | 动词 + 副词；efficient 是形容词 | A clear plan helps students learn efficiently. |
| online libraries | 在线图书馆 | 复数名词短语；也可用 digital libraries | Online libraries give readers access to many books. |
| reliable information | 可靠的信息 | 形容词 + 不可数名词 | Researchers need reliable information. |
| inaccurate content | 不准确的内容 | 形容词 + 名词；content 在此通常不可数 | Inaccurate content can mislead readers. |
| play an important role in | 在……中发挥重要作用 | play a role in + 名词或动名词；复用写作003已有表达 | Public transport plays an important role in reducing pollution. |
| digital tools | 数字工具 | 复数名词短语 | Digital tools can support classroom learning. |
| analyze information | 分析信息 | 动词短语；information 不可数 | Students should analyze information before sharing it. |
| solve problems | 解决问题 | 动词短语；problem 为可数名词 | Teamwork helps students solve problems. |
| master skills | 掌握技能 | 及物动词 + 复数名词 | Regular practice helps learners master new skills. |
| be prepared for challenges | 为挑战做好准备 | be prepared for + 名词；challenges 常用复数 | Volunteers should be prepared for unexpected challenges. |
| protect personal information | 保护个人信息 | protect + 宾语；沿用 protect 的原有词条并补充本题搭配 | Users should protect personal information online. |
| online risks | 网络风险 | 复数名词短语 | Students should learn about online risks. |
| practical training | 实践培训 | 名词短语；training 在此通常不可数 | Practical training helps students apply what they learn. |
| personal development | 个人发展 | 名词短语；development 在此不可数 | Reading can support personal development. |

<a id="writing-patterns-004"></a>
#### 可复用写作句型（8项）

| 英文模板 | 中文用途 | 语法注意事项 | 可迁移例句 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| In my opinion, ... is/are essential for ... | 表达某事的重要性 | 主语单数用 is、复数用 are；for 后接名词或动名词。 | In my opinion, regular exercise is essential for good health. |
| ... plays an important role in ... | 分析某事的作用 | play 随主语变形；in 后接名词或动名词。 | Public transport plays an important role in reducing pollution. |
| For example, ... | 举例支持前文观点 | 例子须与论点相关；句首短语后用逗号。 | For example, schools can organize community cleanups. |
| As ... develops rapidly, ... | 引出技术或社会变化的背景 | as 引导从句；主句须有完整主语与谓语。 | As online education develops rapidly, more students can learn from home. |
| Students who ... will be better prepared for ... | 强调技能带来的未来优势 | who 从句修饰 students；主句用 will be；prepared 后接 for。 | Students who practise teamwork will be better prepared for future jobs. |
| ... is not only ... but also ... | 强调两个并列方面 | not only 与 but also 后的语法成分应平行。 | Reading is not only enjoyable but also useful for learning. |
| Therefore, ... should ... | 根据前文提出建议 | therefore 表示因果；should 后接动词原形。 | Therefore, cities should create more green spaces. |
| Only in this way can ... | 强调只有这样才能达到目标 | 句首 only + 状语引发主句部分倒装：can + 主语 + 动词原形。 | Only in this way can communities reduce waste. |

#### 语法注意事项

1. `these abilities are essential for ...` 的主语是复数，谓语用 `are`；讲单数事物时改用 `is`。`be essential for + 名词/动名词` 与写作003的 `be essential to doing something` 都可用，注意介词后的形式。
2. `Students who master these skills will ...` 中 `who` 引导定语从句；主句谓语为 `will be`。
3. `not only ... but also ...` 两侧保持语法平行；`Only in this way` 置于句首时，主句用 `can + 主语 + 动词原形`。
4. 题目要求的150—200词不含指定句；提交个人作文时仍需重新统计，不直接套用本篇参考范文的词数。

<a id="material-005"></a>
### 005｜2016年6月第1套｜虚拟世界与现实生活

**材料类型：**英语六级写作　　**整理日期：**2026-10-30　　**真题信息：**2016年6月大学英语六级考试写作（第1套）<br>
**真题来源：**[2016年6月六级第1套试卷](https://github.com/0609x/CET46-Resources/blob/main/%E5%85%AD%E7%BA%A7%E7%9C%9F%E9%A2%98/2016.06/%E7%9C%9F%E9%A2%98Word/2016%E5%B9%B406%E6%9C%88%E5%85%AD%E7%BA%A7%E7%9C%9F%E9%A2%98%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC1%E5%A5%97%EF%BC%89.doc)

#### 题目要求概要

围绕“生活在虚拟世界”展开短文，思考人们越来越多地待在虚拟空间、与现实中的人互动减少时可能产生的影响。作答时间30分钟，要求150—200词。此处为题意概要，完整题目请查看真题来源。

#### 写作思路

| 段落 | 内容安排 | 可展开的角度 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第一段 | 引入虚拟世界已融入日常生活，并提出需要关注过度使用的问题 | 在线学习、社交、获取信息都很方便 |
| 第二段 | 分析过度使用的影响 | 面对面交流减少；屏幕使用挤占睡眠、运动和户外活动时间 |
| 第三段 | 承认科技价值，提出平衡方法并总结 | 有目的地使用网络；交流时放下手机；保留线下活动 |

#### 学习参考范文（原创）

Today, the virtual world is an important part of daily life. We study online, keep in touch with friends, and find information with a few clicks. Although these tools are convenient, spending too much time in virtual spaces may weaken our connections with people around us.

One major concern is that online communication cannot fully replace face-to-face interaction. A message may be quick, but it does not always convey tone or emotion. When people constantly look at screens during meals or meetings, they may miss chances to listen carefully and build trust. Excessive screen time can also reduce time for exercise, sleep, and outdoor activities.

However, virtual technology itself is not the problem. It gives people access to education and helps families stay connected across long distances. The key is to use it with purpose. We can set limits on recreational screen time, put phones away when talking with others, and make room for offline activities.

In short, a healthy life requires balance. By using digital tools wisely while protecting real-world relationships, we can enjoy the benefits of the virtual world without becoming isolated from the real one.

**范文词数：**188词。**说明：**本文为原创学习参考稿，不是官方范文，也不代表对考生答案的评分。

#### 中英文逐句对照

| 英文原句 | 中文理解 |
| --- | --- |
| Today, the virtual world is an important part of daily life. | 如今，虚拟世界已成为日常生活的重要组成部分。 |
| We study online, keep in touch with friends, and find information with a few clicks. | 我们在线学习、与朋友保持联系，只需点击几下就能找到信息。 |
| Although these tools are convenient, spending too much time in virtual spaces may weaken our connections with people around us. | 尽管这些工具十分便利，但在虚拟空间里花费过多时间，可能会削弱我们与身边人的联系。 |
| One major concern is that online communication cannot fully replace face-to-face interaction. | 一个主要问题是，线上交流无法完全取代面对面互动。 |
| A message may be quick, but it does not always convey tone or emotion. | 消息虽然快捷，却不一定能传达语气或情绪。 |
| When people constantly look at screens during meals or meetings, they may miss chances to listen carefully and build trust. | 如果人们在用餐或开会时总盯着屏幕，就可能错过认真倾听、建立信任的机会。 |
| Excessive screen time can also reduce time for exercise, sleep, and outdoor activities. | 过多的屏幕时间还会挤占运动、睡眠和户外活动的时间。 |
| However, virtual technology itself is not the problem. | 然而，问题不在于虚拟技术本身。 |
| It gives people access to education and helps families stay connected across long distances. | 它让人们能够接受教育，也帮助相隔遥远的家人保持联系。 |
| The key is to use it with purpose. | 关键在于有目的地使用它。 |
| We can set limits on recreational screen time, put phones away when talking with others, and make room for offline activities. | 我们可以限制娱乐屏幕时间，与人交谈时收起手机，并为线下活动留出时间。 |
| In short, a healthy life requires balance. | 总之，健康的生活需要平衡。 |
| By using digital tools wisely while protecting real-world relationships, we can enjoy the benefits of the virtual world without becoming isolated from the real one. | 明智地使用数字工具，同时维护现实关系，我们就能享受虚拟世界带来的好处，而不至于与现实生活隔绝。 |

#### 核心词汇与搭配

| 词汇／搭配 | 含义 | 用法示例 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| an important part of daily life | 日常生活的重要组成部分 | Technology is an important part of modern life. |
| keep in touch with somebody | 与某人保持联系 | keep in touch with friends online |
| with a few clicks | 只需点击几下 | find useful information with a few clicks |
| spend time in... / spend time doing... | 在……中花时间／花时间做…… | spend time in virtual spaces; spend time talking |
| weaken one's connection with... | 削弱某人与……的联系 | weaken our connection with the real world |
| face-to-face interaction | 面对面互动 | encourage face-to-face interaction |
| convey tone or emotion | 传达语气或情绪 | convey tone and emotion |
| miss a chance to do... | 错过做……的机会 | miss a chance to listen carefully |
| build trust | 建立信任 | build trust through honest communication |
| excessive screen time | 过多的屏幕使用时间 | reduce excessive screen time |
| have access to... | 有机会使用／获得…… | have access to online education |
| stay connected | 保持联系 | help families stay connected |
| set limits on... | 为……设定限制 | set limits on recreational screen time |
| put ... away | 把……收起来 | put phones away during dinner |
| make room for... | 为……留出空间／时间 | make room for offline activities |
| become isolated from... | 与……隔绝 | become isolated from the real world |

#### 可复用写作句型

1. **引出背景：** `Today, [主题] has become an important part of [领域/生活].`
2. **承认优点并提出隐忧：** `Although [事物] brings us [好处], excessive use of it may [负面影响].`
3. **点明主要问题：** `One major concern is that [问题/现象].`
4. **说明影响：** `When people [某行为], they may miss chances to [积极行为/结果].`
5. **补充另一影响：** `[现象] can also reduce the time available for [活动/需求].`
6. **平衡论点：** `However, [事物] itself is not the problem; the key is how we use it.`
7. **提出措施：** `We can [措施一], [措施二], and make room for [积极活动].`
8. **总结观点：** `In short, [主题] requires a balance between [方面A] and [方面B].`
9. **强调合理使用：** `By [doing something] wisely, we can [积极结果] without [不利结果].`
10. **总结行动：** `Both individuals and [组织/机构] should take action to [目标].`

使用时按题目替换主题词和具体做法，并检查主谓一致、名词单复数和搭配；不要为了套句而添加与题意无关的内容。

#### 写作高频词汇

| 词汇 | 常见含义 | 常用搭配 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| affect | 影响（动词） | affect our health and relationships |
| benefit | 好处；使受益 | bring benefits to students |
| concern | 担忧；关注的问题 | One major concern is that... |
| contribute | 促成；贡献 | contribute to better communication |
| encourage | 鼓励；促进 | encourage people to communicate in person |
| improve | 改善；提高 | improve the quality of life |
| increase | 增加；增长 | increase access to information |
| reduce | 减少；降低 | reduce stress / screen time |
| provide | 提供 | provide people with useful resources |
| replace | 取代 | replace face-to-face communication |
| require | 需要；要求 | require time and effort |
| support | 支持；支撑 | support students' learning |
| balance | 平衡 | maintain a balance between work and rest |
| connection | 联系；连接 | strengthen connections with others |
| opportunity | 机会 | create more opportunities for learning |
| relationship | 关系 | maintain healthy relationships |
| responsibility | 责任 | take responsibility for one's choices |
| technology | 技术 | use technology wisely |
| well-being | 身心健康；幸福 | protect people's well-being |
| robot | 机器人 | industrial robots; robots in healthcare |
| automation | 自动化 | the growth of automation |
| empathy | 同理心 | empathy and trust |

**易混提醒：**`affect` 通常作动词“影响”；`effect` 多作名词“影响、效果”。`increase / reduce` 后可接名词；描述某事导致某结果时，常用 `contribute to + 名词／动名词`。

#### 关键语法与易错点

1. **让步与转折。** `Although these tools are convenient, ...` 先承认便利，再提出隐忧；使用 `Although` 时，不要再在同一句中加 `but`。
2. **动名词短语作主语。** `Spending too much time ... may weaken...` 中，动名词短语作主语；情态动词 `may` 后接动词原形 `weaken`。
3. **`spend time` 的结构。** 花时间做事用 `spend time doing`；在某处花时间可用 `spend time in a place`。不写 `spend time to do`。
4. **`replace` 的搭配。** `replace A` 表示取代A；`replace A with B` 才表示“用B替换A”。
5. **使役与促使结构。** `allow somebody to do` 保留 `to`；`help somebody (to) do` 两种都可以；`make somebody do` 不加 `to`。
6. **`the key is to...`。** `The key is to use technology wisely.` 是常见建议表达，`to` 后接动词原形；`The key is using technology wisely` 也成立。
7. **`by` 后接动名词。** `By using digital tools wisely, ...` 中 `by` 是介词，后接 `using`；句首方式状语的逻辑执行者应与主句主语一致。
8. **`too much` 与 `too many`。** `time` 是不可数名词，用 `too much time`；可数名词复数用 `too many`，如 `too many messages`。
9. **`access` 的固定搭配。** `have access to + 名词` 中的 `to` 是介词，后接名词或动名词，不直接接动词原形。
10. **`set limits on`。** `on` 是介词，后接名词或动名词：`set limits on screen time / spending time online`。
11. **`put something away` 的语序。** 名词可写 `put phones away` 或 `put away the phones`；代词必须放在中间：`put them away`。
12. **并列结构保持一致。** `set limits..., put phones away..., and make room...` 三项都是动词短语，形式平行。
13. **`without` 后接名词或动名词。** `without becoming isolated` 中 `without` 是介词；不写 `without become`。
14. **冠词与词性。** `an important part` 中 `important` 以元音音素开头，用 `an`；`excessive` 是形容词，`excessively` 是副词。

#### 迁移练习

1. 过多使用手机可能会减少人们与家人面对面交流的时间。
2. 关键是有目的地使用网络，并为运动和休息留出时间。
3. 明智地使用数字工具，我们就能保持联系而不忽视现实生活。

<details>
<summary>参考表达</summary>

1. Excessive phone use may reduce the time people spend talking face to face with their families.
2. The key is to use the Internet with purpose and make room for exercise and rest.
3. By using digital tools wisely, we can stay connected without neglecting real life.

</details>

**复习标记（用户填写）：**□ 未背　□ 初背　□ 需复习　□ 已能迁移造句<br>

<a id="material-006"></a>
### 006｜2016年6月第2套｜机器人与人类生活

**材料类型：**英语六级写作　　**整理日期：**2026-11-02　　**真题信息：**2016年6月大学英语六级考试写作（第2套）<br>
**真题来源：**[2016年6月六级第2套试卷](https://github.com/0609x/CET46-Resources/blob/main/%E5%85%AD%E7%BA%A7%E7%9C%9F%E9%A2%98/2016.06/%E7%9C%9F%E9%A2%98Word/2016%E5%B9%B406%E6%9C%88%E5%85%AD%E7%BA%A7%E7%9C%9F%E9%A2%98%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC2%E5%A5%97%EF%BC%89.doc)

#### 题目要求概要

围绕机器人在工业和日常生活中的使用展开短文，思考随着机器人承担更多原由人类完成的工作，可能带来的影响。作答时间30分钟，要求150—200词。此处为题意概要，完整题目请查看真题来源。

#### 写作思路

| 段落 | 内容安排 | 可展开的角度 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第一段 | 引入机器人应用增加，提出其影响有利有弊 | 工厂、家庭、医疗与公共服务 |
| 第二段 | 说明机器人带来的益处 | 承担重复、危险或体力要求高的工作；提高效率；辅助有需要的人 |
| 第三段 | 分析挑战并提出平衡做法 | 部分岗位变化、员工需要培训；保留需要同理心和沟通的工作；让技术服务于人 |

#### 学习参考范文（原创）

Robots are no longer limited to factories; they are increasingly used in homes, hospitals, and public services. As technology improves, they may take over more tasks that people currently perform. This change can bring both benefits and challenges.

On the one hand, robots can handle repetitive, dangerous, or physically demanding work. They can operate in environments unsafe for humans and perform precise tasks without tiring easily. As a result, businesses may become more efficient, while workers can focus on work that requires judgment, creativity, and communication. Robots can also assist older adults and people with disabilities in daily life.

On the other hand, replacing people too quickly may cause job losses and leave some workers unprepared for new roles. Human contact also matters in fields such as education and healthcare, where empathy and trust are essential. Therefore, society should introduce robots responsibly, provide training for workers, and ensure that technology supports rather than simply replaces people. With careful planning, robots can improve our lives while humans remain responsible for decisions that require understanding and compassion.

**范文词数：**175词。**说明：**本文为原创学习参考稿，不是官方范文，也不代表对考生答案的评分。

#### 中英文逐句对照

| 英文原句 | 中文理解 |
| --- | --- |
| Robots are no longer limited to factories; they are increasingly used in homes, hospitals, and public services. | 机器人已不再局限于工厂；它们正越来越多地应用于家庭、医院和公共服务领域。 |
| As technology improves, they may take over more tasks that people currently perform. | 随着技术进步，机器人可能会接手更多目前由人类完成的任务。 |
| This change can bring both benefits and challenges. | 这一变化既会带来好处，也会带来挑战。 |
| On the one hand, robots can handle repetitive, dangerous, or physically demanding work. | 一方面，机器人可以承担重复、危险或对体力要求很高的工作。 |
| They can operate in environments unsafe for humans and perform precise tasks without tiring easily. | 它们可以在对人类不安全的环境中作业，并完成精细任务而不易疲劳。 |
| As a result, businesses may become more efficient, while workers can focus on work that requires judgment, creativity, and communication. | 因此，企业可能会变得更有效率，而员工则可以专注于需要判断力、创造力和沟通能力的工作。 |
| Robots can also assist older adults and people with disabilities in daily life. | 机器人还可以在日常生活中帮助老年人和残障人士。 |
| On the other hand, replacing people too quickly may cause job losses and leave some workers unprepared for new roles. | 另一方面，过快地取代人工可能导致失业，并使一些员工无法为新的岗位做好准备。 |
| Human contact also matters in fields such as education and healthcare, where empathy and trust are essential. | 在教育和医疗等领域，人际接触也很重要，因为同理心和信任不可或缺。 |
| Therefore, society should introduce robots responsibly, provide training for workers, and ensure that technology supports rather than simply replaces people. | 因此，社会应负责任地引入机器人，为员工提供培训，并确保技术是支持人类，而非单纯取代人类。 |
| With careful planning, robots can improve our lives while humans remain responsible for decisions that require understanding and compassion. | 通过周密规划，机器人能够改善我们的生活，同时由人类继续负责那些需要理解力和同情心的决策。 |

#### 核心词汇与搭配

| 词汇／搭配 | 含义 | 用法示例 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| be limited to... | 局限于…… | Robots are no longer limited to factory work. |
| be increasingly used in... | 越来越多地用于…… | Robots are increasingly used in healthcare. |
| take over a task | 接手一项任务 | Machines can take over repetitive tasks. |
| bring both benefits and challenges | 既带来好处也带来挑战 | New technology can bring both benefits and challenges. |
| on the one hand / on the other hand | 一方面／另一方面 | On the one hand, automation saves time; on the other hand, it changes jobs. |
| handle repetitive work | 处理重复性工作 | Robots can handle repetitive work in factories. |
| physically demanding | 对体力要求高的 | Construction can involve physically demanding tasks. |
| operate in an environment | 在某种环境中运行／作业 | Robots can operate in dangerous environments. |
| perform precise tasks | 执行精细任务 | Machines can perform precise tasks consistently. |
| as a result | 因此；结果 | As a result, production may become more efficient. |
| focus on... | 专注于…… | Workers can focus on creative work. |
| require judgment / creativity | 需要判断力／创造力 | Some decisions require human judgment. |
| assist somebody with... | 帮助某人处理…… | Robots can assist older adults with daily tasks. |
| be unprepared for... | 对……没有准备 | Workers may be unprepared for changes in the job market. |
| empathy and trust | 同理心与信任 | Education depends on empathy and trust. |
| provide training for... | 为……提供培训 | Employers should provide training for workers. |
| rather than doing... | 而不是做…… | Technology should support people rather than replace them. |
| with careful planning | 通过周密规划 | With careful planning, new systems can benefit everyone. |
| remain responsible for... | 继续对……负责 | Humans should remain responsible for important decisions. |

#### 可复用写作句型

1. **引入变化：** `As [technology / society] develops, [change] is becoming increasingly common.`
2. **表达双重影响：** `This change can bring both benefits and challenges.`
3. **平衡列举：** `On the one hand, [benefit]. On the other hand, [challenge].`
4. **说明技术优势：** `[Technology] can handle [task], allowing people to focus on [human-centered work].`
5. **解释结果：** `As a result, [positive outcome], while [another group] can [related benefit].`
6. **说明风险：** `If [change] happens too quickly, it may [negative consequence].`
7. **提出责任做法：** `Society should [action], provide [support], and ensure that [principle].`
8. **作出平衡结论：** `With careful planning, [technology] can [benefit] while people remain responsible for [important decisions].`

使用时根据题意调整主语、时态和论据；`while` 可表示对比或同时发生，须让逻辑关系清楚。

#### 写作高频词汇

| 词汇 | 常见含义 | 常用搭配 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| robot | 机器人 | industrial robots; robots in healthcare |
| task | 任务 | perform / handle a task |
| efficient / efficiency | 有效率的／效率 | improve efficiency; work efficiently |
| replace | 取代 | replace human workers; replace A with B |
| challenge | 挑战 | face new challenges |
| assist | 帮助；协助 | assist older adults with daily tasks |
| require | 需要；要求 | require judgment and creativity |
| provide | 提供 | provide training for workers |
| responsible | 负责任的 | use technology responsibly |
| empathy | 同理心 | empathy and trust |
| employment | 就业 | changes in employment |
| automation | 自动化 | the growth of automation |

#### 关键语法与易错点

1. **`no longer` 的位置。** `be no longer limited to...` 中 `no longer` 通常放在 be 动词后、主要成分前；也可写 `no longer do...`。
2. **被动语态。** `are increasingly used` 是一般现在时被动结构，表示机器人正越来越多地被使用；`used` 前需要 be 动词。
3. **`as` 引导时间或变化背景。** `As technology improves, ...` 表示“随着技术进步”；从句主语是单数 `technology`，谓语用 `improves`。
4. **`take over` 与 `replace`。** `take over tasks` 强调接手任务；`replace people` 强调取代某人。按句意选择，不要写 `replace to people`。
5. **形容词并列修饰。** `repetitive, dangerous, or physically demanding work` 中多个形容词共同修饰 `work`；`work` 在此为不可数名词。
6. **`without` 后接动名词。** `without tiring easily` 中 `without` 是介词，后接 `-ing`，不写 `without tire`。
7. **结果与对比。** `As a result` 引出结果；`while` 在本句中对比企业效率与员工工作的变化。避免把仅表示时间的 `while` 误用成因果词。
8. **定语从句。** `work that requires judgment...` 中 `that` 指代不可数名词 `work`，从句谓语用单数 `requires`。
9. **`assist` 的搭配。** 可写 `assist somebody with something` 或 `assist somebody in doing something`；也可写 `assist somebody to do something`。避免把 `assist` 与 `help` 的结构混为一谈。
10. **`leave somebody + 形容词`。** `leave some workers unprepared` 表示“使一些员工处于未准备好的状态”；`unprepared` 是宾语补足语。
11. **非限制性／补充性关系从句。** `fields such as education and healthcare, where empathy...` 中 `where` 指这些领域，可理解为“在这些领域中”。也可另起一句，避免复杂句失控。
12. **`rather than` 的平行结构。** `technology supports rather than simply replaces people` 中，`supports` 与 `replaces` 都是第三人称单数谓语，结构平行。`rather than` 后也可以接名词或动名词，按句子结构选择。
13. **`ensure that` 引导宾语从句。** `ensure that technology supports people` 中 `that` 后使用完整的主谓结构；`technology` 为单数，谓语用 `supports`。
14. **`while` 从句中的主语。** `robots can improve our lives while humans remain responsible...` 两个分句主语不同，均需明确写出，避免误读为同一主体。

#### 迁移练习

1. 自动化可以承担危险的工作，让员工专注于需要创造力的任务。
2. 如果工作岗位变化过快，一些员工可能无法为新要求做好准备。
3. 社会应为员工提供培训，并确保技术支持而非单纯取代人类。

<details>
<summary>参考表达</summary>

1. Automation can handle dangerous work, allowing workers to focus on tasks that require creativity.
2. If jobs change too quickly, some workers may be unprepared for new requirements.
3. Society should provide training for workers and ensure that technology supports rather than simply replaces people.

</details>

**复习标记（用户填写）：**□ 未背　□ 初背　□ 需复习　□ 已能迁移造句<br>
**个人作文／备注：**尚未提供个人作文；本条范文仅为原创学习参考稿。

<a id="material-007"></a>
### 007｜2024年6月第3套｜独立学习能力

**材料类型：**六级写作议论文　　**英文主题：**Independent Learning Ability　　**学习日期：**2026-10-25　　**题目来源：**按本次提供的真题图片与题目文字整理。

#### 真题信息与题目要求

> **Directions:** For this part, you are allowed 30 minutes to write an essay that begins with the sentence: “Nowadays, cultivating independent learning ability is becoming increasingly crucial for personal development.” You can make comments, cite examples or use your personal experiences to develop your essay. You should write at least 150 words but no more than 200 words (not including the sentence given).

**作答要求：**指定开头句必须原样放在正文开头；除该句外正文150—200词；写作时间30分钟。本章收录的是学习参考范文，不是官方范文或个人独立完成的原始作文。

#### 学习参考范文

**The Importance of Independent Learning Ability**

Nowadays, cultivating independent learning ability is becoming increasingly crucial for personal development. In my view, it helps students respond to new challenges instead of waiting for others to provide every answer.

First, independent learners can identify what they do not understand and find suitable ways to improve. For example, when a student struggles with English listening, they can set a weekly goal, choose reliable materials, and review mistakes after each practice session. This process often makes learning more effective than simply repeating exercises without reflection. Second, the ability to learn independently remains valuable after graduation. New technologies change the skills required at work, so employees need to update their knowledge and adapt to unfamiliar tasks. People who know how to learn are better prepared to solve problems and pursue new opportunities.

To develop this ability, students should start with small, realistic plans. They can track their progress, ask for help when necessary, and adjust their methods according to the results. Schools can also encourage students to explore questions on their own. With steady practice, independent learning can become a lifelong habit that supports both academic progress and personal growth.

**程序字数统计：**指定开头句12词；其余正文177词；全文189词。统计使用英文字母、撇号和连字符识别英文单词，标题不计入词数。按题目规定排除指定开头句后，正文符合150—200词要求。

#### 中英文逐句对照（12句）

1. **英文：**Nowadays, cultivating independent learning ability is becoming increasingly crucial for personal development.<br>
   **中文：**如今，培养独立学习能力对个人发展变得越来越重要。

2. **英文：**In my view, it helps students respond to new challenges instead of waiting for others to provide every answer.<br>
   **中文：**我认为，它能帮助学生应对新挑战，而不是等待别人提供所有答案。

3. **英文：**First, independent learners can identify what they do not understand and find suitable ways to improve.<br>
   **中文：**首先，独立学习者能够发现自己不理解的内容，并找到合适的改进方法。

4. **英文：**For example, when a student struggles with English listening, they can set a weekly goal, choose reliable materials, and review mistakes after each practice session.<br>
   **中文：**例如，当学生在英语听力方面遇到困难时，他们可以设定每周目标、选择可靠材料，并在每次练习后复盘错误。

5. **英文：**This process often makes learning more effective than simply repeating exercises without reflection.<br>
   **中文：**与不加反思地重复练习相比，这一过程往往能使学习更加有效。

6. **英文：**Second, the ability to learn independently remains valuable after graduation.<br>
   **中文：**其次，独立学习能力在毕业后仍然很有价值。

7. **英文：**New technologies change the skills required at work, so employees need to update their knowledge and adapt to unfamiliar tasks.<br>
   **中文：**新技术改变了工作所需的技能，因此员工需要更新知识并适应不熟悉的任务。

8. **英文：**People who know how to learn are better prepared to solve problems and pursue new opportunities.<br>
   **中文：**懂得如何学习的人更能做好解决问题和把握新机会的准备。

9. **英文：**To develop this ability, students should start with small, realistic plans.<br>
   **中文：**为了培养这种能力，学生应从小而切实可行的计划开始。

10. **英文：**They can track their progress, ask for help when necessary, and adjust their methods according to the results.<br>
    **中文：**他们可以记录进度，在必要时寻求帮助，并根据结果调整方法。

11. **英文：**Schools can also encourage students to explore questions on their own.<br>
    **中文：**学校也可以鼓励学生自主探索问题。

12. **英文：**With steady practice, independent learning can become a lifelong habit that supports both academic progress and personal growth.<br>
    **中文：**通过持续练习，独立学习可以成为一种终身习惯，促进学业进步和个人成长。

#### 三段式结构分析

1. **第一段：引出主题。**原样使用指定开头句，再用 In my view, ... 表明观点，说明独立学习能够帮助学生应对新挑战。
2. **第二段：分析作用。**先写发现知识缺口、制定目标、选择材料与复盘错误，再说明独立学习在毕业后仍能帮助人们更新知识、适应任务并把握机会；英语听力练习的例子直接服务于第一个论点。
3. **第三段：建议与总结。**建议从小而可行的计划开始，持续记录进度、必要时求助、按结果调整方法，并由学校鼓励自主探索，最终将独立学习培养为终身习惯。

#### 核心词汇与搭配（26项）

本表是写作007的主题学习视图。personal development 复用写作004已经出现的表达，其余25项按本题语境新增；主题视图用于集中复习，不在文末长期总表重复建行，也不代表本人已经掌握。

| 英语表达 | 中文释义 | 词性或结构说明 | 可复用英文例句 | 例句中文 | 背诵优先级 | 来源材料编号 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| independent learning ability | 独立学习能力 | 名词短语；ability 在此通常用单数 | Independent learning ability helps students face change. | 独立学习能力帮助学生面对变化。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| personal development | 个人发展 | 名词短语；复用写作004已有表达 | Reading can contribute to personal development. | 阅读能够促进个人发展。 | 优先背 | 写作004、写作007 |
| respond to new challenges | 应对新挑战 | respond to + 名词；不能漏掉 to | Young people need to respond to new challenges. | 年轻人需要应对新挑战。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| independent learner | 独立学习者 | 可数名词；复数为 independent learners | An independent learner knows how to find useful information. | 独立学习者懂得如何寻找有用信息。 | 拓展背 | 写作007 |
| identify what one does not understand | 发现自己不理解的内容 | identify + what 引导的宾语从句 | Students should identify what they do not understand. | 学生应发现自己不理解的内容。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| find suitable ways to improve | 找到合适的改进方法 | a way to do；suitable 修饰 ways | Learners can find suitable ways to improve. | 学习者可以找到合适的改进方法。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| struggle with | 在……方面感到困难 | struggle with + 名词或动名词 | Some students struggle with time management. | 一些学生在时间管理方面存在困难。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| set a weekly goal | 设定每周目标 | set + goal；goal 为可数名词 | I set a weekly goal for vocabulary review. | 我为词汇复习设定了每周目标。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| reliable materials | 可靠的学习材料 | 形容词 + 复数名词 | Students should choose reliable materials. | 学生应选择可靠的学习材料。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| review mistakes | 复盘错误 | review + 复数名词 | Reviewing mistakes can improve accuracy. | 复盘错误能够提高准确性。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| practice session | 一次练习 | 可数名词短语；复数为 practice sessions | She takes notes after each practice session. | 她在每次练习后记笔记。 | 拓展背 | 写作007 |
| without reflection | 不加反思 | without 是介词；后接名词或动名词 | Repeating a task without reflection may waste time. | 不加反思地重复任务可能浪费时间。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| remain valuable | 仍然有价值 | remain + 形容词 | Communication skills remain valuable at work. | 沟通技能在工作中仍然很有价值。 | 拓展背 | 写作007 |
| update one's knowledge | 更新某人的知识 | one's 随主语变化 | Workers need to update their knowledge regularly. | 员工需要定期更新知识。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| adapt to unfamiliar tasks | 适应不熟悉的任务 | adapt to + 名词；to 是介词 | New employees must adapt to unfamiliar tasks. | 新员工必须适应不熟悉的任务。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| be prepared to do something | 为做某事做好准备 | be prepared to + 动词原形；区别于 be prepared for + 名词 | Graduates should be prepared to learn new skills. | 毕业生应做好学习新技能的准备。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| pursue new opportunities | 把握／追求新的机会 | pursue 是及物动词，直接接宾语 | Young people should be willing to pursue new opportunities. | 年轻人应愿意把握新的机会。 | 拓展背 | 写作007 |
| realistic plans | 切实可行的计划 | 形容词 + 复数名词 | Realistic plans are easier to follow. | 切实可行的计划更容易执行。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| track one's progress | 记录某人的进度 | one's 随主语变化 | A learning diary can help students track their progress. | 学习日志可以帮助学生记录进度。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| adjust one's methods | 调整某人的方法 | adjust + 宾语；one's 随主语变化 | Learners should adjust their methods when necessary. | 学习者应在必要时调整方法。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| according to the results | 根据结果 | according to + 名词短语 | We can improve the plan according to the results. | 我们可以根据结果改进计划。 | 拓展背 | 写作007 |
| explore questions on one's own | 独立探索问题 | on one's own 表示独自、自主地 | Students need time to explore questions on their own. | 学生需要时间自主探索问题。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| with steady practice | 通过持续练习 | with + 名词短语表示条件或方式 | With steady practice, speaking becomes easier. | 通过持续练习，口语会变得更容易。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| lifelong habit | 终身习惯 | 可数名词短语 | Reading can become a lifelong habit. | 阅读可以成为一种终身习惯。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |
| academic progress | 学业进步 | 不可数名词短语 | Regular review supports academic progress. | 定期复习有助于学业进步。 | 拓展背 | 写作007 |
| personal growth | 个人成长 | 不可数名词短语 | Volunteering can promote personal growth. | 志愿服务能够促进个人成长。 | 优先背 | 写作007 |

<a id="writing-patterns-007"></a>
#### 可复用写作句型（10项）

| 英文结构 | 中文作用 | 语法说明 | 可迁移例句 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ... is becoming increasingly crucial for ... | 表示某事对某个目标越来越重要 | increasingly 修饰形容词 crucial；for 后接名词。 | Environmental awareness is becoming increasingly crucial for sustainable development. |
| In my view, ... helps somebody do something. | 表达个人观点及某事的作用 | help + 人 + 动词原形；也可用 help somebody to do。 | In my view, regular exercise helps people stay healthy. |
| ... can identify what ... and find suitable ways to ... | 表达发现问题并寻找解决方法 | what 引导宾语从句；两个并列谓语保持形式一致。 | Researchers can identify what causes the problem and find suitable ways to solve it. |
| For example, when ..., somebody can ... | 用具体情境支持论点 | when 引导时间或情境从句；例子必须支持前文观点。 | For example, when residents notice waste, they can report it to the community. |
| This process makes ... more effective than ... | 比较两种方法的效果 | make + 宾语 + 形容词；than 前后比较对象须对应。 | This process makes communication more effective than sending unclear messages. |
| As technology develops, ... | 引出技术发展带来的变化 | as + 完整从句在此表示“随着”。 | As technology develops, public services become more convenient. |
| People who ... are better prepared to ... | 使用定语从句说明某类人的优势 | who 修饰 people；be prepared to 后接动词原形。 | People who exercise regularly are better prepared to handle stress. |
| To develop this ability, somebody should ... | 用目的状语提出建议 | 句首不定式表示目的，其逻辑主语应与主句主语一致。 | To develop communication skills, students should join group discussions. |
| ... according to the results. | 表示根据结果调整行动 | according to 后接名词或名词短语。 | Teachers can adjust the lesson according to the results. |
| With steady practice, ... can become ... | 表示通过持续练习形成能力或习惯 | with + 名词短语表示条件或伴随背景。 | With steady practice, public speaking can become less stressful. |

#### 重点语法提醒

1. help somebody do something 中 help 后可接动词原形，也可接 to do。
2. struggle with 后接名词或动名词；adapt to 中 to 是介词，同样接名词或动名词。
3. People who ... 中 who 引导定语从句；To develop this ability 是目的状语，其逻辑主语应与主句一致。
4. instead of 后接名词、代词或动名词；more effective than 前后的比较内容要保持逻辑一致。
5. be prepared to do something 表示准备做某事；be prepared for something 后接名词，两种结构不要混淆。

#### 重点可复用句子

以下句子用于学习和迁移，不表示本人已经独立掌握。

| 句子或模板 | 可替换部分 |
| --- | --- |
| ... helps people respond to new challenges instead of waiting for others to provide every answer. | 将主语替换为 Independent learning、Teamwork 或其他能够帮助人们应对问题的事物。 |
| ... can identify what they do not understand and find suitable ways to improve. | 替换主语和 what 从句，也可把 improve 换成具体行动。 |
| This process often makes learning more effective than simply repeating exercises without reflection. | 替换 learning、比较对象及具体过程。 |
| People who know how to learn are better prepared to solve problems and pursue new opportunities. | 替换 who 从句及两个并列动作。 |
| With steady practice, ... can become a lifelong habit that supports both academic progress and personal growth. | 替换习惯、能力以及 both ... and ... 后的两个结果。 |

#### 学习状态

本章已整理题目、原创学习参考范文、中英文对照、词汇、句型和语法提醒。尚未收录个人独立作文，本章不代表个人写作成果。

### 008｜2024年12月第1套｜现实目标与持续努力

**材料类型：**英语六级写作　　**整理日期：**2026-11-06　　**真题信息：**2024年12月大学英语六级考试写作（第1套）

#### 真题信息与题目要求

> **Directions:** For this part, you are allowed 30 minutes to write an essay that begins with the sentence “To increase the likelihood of success, one should set realistic goals and work persistently towards them.” You can make comments, cite examples or use your personal experiences to develop your essay. You should write at least 150 words but no more than 200 words. You should copy the sentence given in quotes at the beginning of your essay.

**中文理解：**围绕“切合实际的目标与持续努力如何提高成功的可能性”展开论述。指定句必须原样置于开头；作答时间30分钟，全文150—200词。

#### 审题与写作逻辑

| 段落 | 推进任务 | 避免的问题 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第一段 | 解释现实目标为何能提供方向和可执行步骤 | 只说“目标重要”，不解释“现实”体现在哪里 |
| 第二段 | 说明坚持如何把计划转化为进步，并用学习或技能训练举例 | 把坚持写成盲目重复，不写反馈和调整 |
| 第三段 | 总结“目标、行动、复盘”的循环 | 只喊口号，没有具体方法 |

#### 原创学习参考范文

To increase the likelihood of success, one should set realistic goals and work persistently towards them. A realistic goal gives people a clear direction while keeping the task manageable. Instead of promising to master a subject in a week, a student can divide it into smaller objectives and complete one step at a time.

Persistence is equally important because meaningful progress rarely appears overnight. For example, a student preparing for an English test may plan to review vocabulary, practise listening, and analyse mistakes every week. Some days will bring little improvement, but regular effort gradually reveals weaknesses and builds confidence. When a method proves ineffective, persistence should also include reflection and adjustment rather than mechanical repetition.

Therefore, goals and persistence should work together. We can write down specific targets, check our progress regularly, and revise the plan when circumstances change. Small achievements then become evidence that the larger goal is possible. By choosing a practical direction and continuing to act on it, we are more likely to turn ambition into lasting results.

**范文词数：**172词。**说明：**本文为Codex原创学习示例，不是官方范文，也不是用户个人作答。

#### 重点句拆解

| 句子 | 结构与作用 |
| --- | --- |
| A realistic goal gives people a clear direction while keeping the task manageable. | `while doing`补充同步效果，把“方向”和“可执行性”连在一起。 |
| Persistence should also include reflection and adjustment rather than mechanical repetition. | `A rather than B`界定真正的坚持，避免把坚持误写成盲目重复。 |

#### 可迁移句型

| 句型骨架 | 中文作用 | 使用限制 | 替换例句及中文 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Instead of promising to..., somebody can... | 用可执行办法替代空泛承诺 | `instead of`后接名词或动名词 | Instead of waiting for motivation, students can begin with a ten-minute task. 与其等待动力，学生可以先做一项十分钟的任务。 |
| When a method proves ineffective, ... should include ... rather than ... | 说明遇到问题时如何调整 | `prove`在此为系动词，后接形容词 | When a plan proves ineffective, improvement should include adjustment rather than denial. 当计划无效时，改进应包括调整而非否认。 |
| By choosing... and continuing to..., we are more likely to... | 总结方法与结果 | `by`后用动名词，并列形式一致 | By reviewing and revising regularly, we are more likely to retain new knowledge. 通过定期复习和修正，我们更可能记住新知识。 |

#### 主题词汇与搭配

| 表达 | 词性及含义 | 常用搭配 | 易错点 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| realistic goal | 名词短语；切合实际的目标 | set a realistic goal | `realistic`不是“真实存在的”唯一含义，此处强调可实现 |
| manageable | adj. 可处理的 | keep a task manageable | 不写成`management`替代形容词 |
| meaningful progress | 名词短语；实质性进步 | make meaningful progress | `progress`通常不可数 |
| mechanical repetition | 名词短语；机械重复 | avoid mechanical repetition | `mechanical`在此表示不加思考 |
| revise a plan | 动词短语；修订计划 | revise the plan regularly | `revise`可表示修改计划或复习内容，依语境判断 |

#### 迁移练习

1. 与其追求不切实际的速度，学习者可以把长期目标拆成每周任务。
2. 持续努力还应包括定期复盘和调整方法。

<details><summary>参考表达</summary>

1. Instead of pursuing unrealistic speed, learners can divide a long-term goal into weekly tasks.
2. Persistent effort should also include regular reflection and adjustment of methods.

</details>

### 009｜2024年12月第2套｜自律与个人成长

**材料类型：**英语六级写作　　**整理日期：**2026-11-06　　**真题信息：**2024年12月大学英语六级考试写作（第2套）

#### 真题信息与题目要求

> **Directions:** For this part, you are allowed 30 minutes to write an essay that begins with the sentence “Nowadays, more and more students have realized the importance of self-discipline in their personal growth.” You can make comments, cite examples or use your personal experiences to develop your essay. You should write at least 150 words but no more than 200 words. You should copy the sentence given in quotes at the beginning of your essay.

**中文理解：**论述自律如何促进学生的个人成长。指定句必须置于开头；作答时间30分钟，全文150—200词。

#### 审题与写作逻辑

| 段落 | 推进任务 | 避免的问题 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第一段 | 界定自律：在缺少监督时仍按计划行动 | 把自律等同于压抑一切兴趣 |
| 第二段 | 从学习、时间管理或健康习惯说明作用 | 罗列多个例子却没有解释成长机制 |
| 第三段 | 提出从小习惯开始、记录和修正的方法 | 只写“我们应当自律” |

#### 原创学习参考范文

Nowadays, more and more students have realized the importance of self-discipline in their personal growth. Self-discipline means acting according to a reasonable plan even when no one is watching. It helps students manage limited time and resist distractions that offer immediate pleasure but little long-term value.

Consider a student who wants to improve academic performance while staying healthy. If the student follows a study schedule, puts the phone away during focused work, and exercises regularly, progress becomes easier to maintain. More importantly, self-discipline teaches responsibility. Each completed task shows that choices have consequences, while each failure provides information for improving the next plan. It does not require a perfect record or the rejection of all entertainment.

Students can develop self-discipline by starting with one small habit, such as reviewing notes for twenty minutes each evening. They should track the habit, identify obstacles, and make reasonable adjustments. Over time, repeated responsible choices strengthen confidence and independence. In this sense, self-discipline is not a restriction on personal growth but a practical means of achieving it.

**范文词数：**173词。**说明：**本文为Codex原创学习示例，不是官方范文，也不是用户个人作答。

#### 重点句拆解

| 句子 | 结构与作用 |
| --- | --- |
| Self-discipline means acting according to a reasonable plan even when no one is watching. | 用`means doing`给概念下定义，`even when`强调无人监督的情境。 |
| It does not require a perfect record or the rejection of all entertainment. | 排除极端理解，使论证更准确。 |

#### 可迁移句型

| 句型骨架 | 中文作用 | 使用限制 | 替换例句及中文 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ... means doing... even when... | 给抽象品质下定义 | `mean`表示“意味着”时后接动名词 | Responsibility means keeping a promise even when doing so is inconvenient. 责任意味着即使不方便也要信守承诺。 |
| Each..., while each... | 对照两类结果 | 两个`each`后的单数名词配单数谓语 | Each success builds confidence, while each mistake offers a lesson. 每次成功都增强信心，而每次错误都提供教训。 |
| ... is not a restriction on... but a practical means of... | 纠正常见误解并重申观点 | `means`在此为单数名词“手段” | Planning is not a restriction on creativity but a practical means of protecting time for it. 规划不是对创造力的限制，而是为创造活动保障时间的实用手段。 |

#### 主题词汇与搭配

| 表达 | 词性及含义 | 常用搭配 | 易错点 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| self-discipline | n. 自律 | develop self-discipline | 通常不可数，不写`a self-discipline` |
| resist distractions | 动词短语；抵制干扰 | resist online distractions | `resist`后直接接名词或动名词 |
| focused work | 名词短语；专注学习或工作 | during focused work | `focused`描述专注状态 |
| track a habit | 动词短语；记录习惯执行情况 | track a daily habit | 不等同于“追踪某人” |
| repeated responsible choices | 名词短语；反复作出的负责选择 | make responsible choices | `choice`可数，按语境使用复数 |

#### 迁移练习

1. 自律并不要求完美，而是要求人在分心后回到计划。
2. 每个小习惯都能为长期成长提供稳定基础。

<details><summary>参考表达</summary>

1. Self-discipline does not require perfection; it requires returning to the plan after a distraction.
2. Each small habit can provide a stable foundation for long-term growth.

</details>

### 010｜2024年12月第3套｜大学探索与人生方向

**材料类型：**英语六级写作　　**整理日期：**2026-11-06　　**真题信息：**2024年12月大学英语六级考试写作（第3套）

#### 真题信息与题目要求

> **Directions:** For this part, you are allowed 30 minutes to write an essay that begins with the sentence “College provides a great opportunity for students to explore various possibilities and find the right path for themselves.” You can make comments, cite examples or use your personal experiences to develop your essay. You should write at least 150 words but no more than 200 words. You should copy the sentence given in quotes at the beginning of your essay.

**中文理解：**论述大学如何帮助学生探索多种可能并找到适合自己的发展道路。指定句必须置于开头；作答时间30分钟，全文150—200词。

#### 审题与写作逻辑

| 段落 | 推进任务 | 避免的问题 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第一段 | 说明大学提供课程、活动和人际交流等探索空间 | 把“探索”写成频繁换目标、毫无积累 |
| 第二段 | 用选课、项目、社团或实习说明如何认识兴趣和能力 | 只列资源，不写这些经历如何帮助判断方向 |
| 第三段 | 强调探索需要反思、咨询和实际尝试 | 把“正确道路”写成唯一且永久不变的答案 |

#### 原创学习参考范文

College provides a great opportunity for students to explore various possibilities and find the right path for themselves. Unlike a fixed route, college life offers courses, projects, clubs, and conversations with people from different backgrounds. These experiences allow students to test interests that were previously only vague ideas.

Exploration becomes valuable when it is connected with reflection. A student interested in data analysis, for instance, may take a statistics course, join a research project, and speak with professionals. Through these activities, the student can discover both enjoyment and areas that require further improvement. An unsuccessful attempt is also useful because it can reveal what kind of work does not match one's strengths or values.

However, exploring possibilities does not mean changing direction without purpose. Students should keep records of what they learn, ask teachers and classmates for honest feedback, and gain practical experience whenever possible. The right path may change as people grow, but informed choices are better than passive decisions. By exploring actively and reflecting carefully, students can leave college with a clearer sense of direction and the ability to adjust it responsibly.

**范文词数：**184词。**说明：**本文为Codex原创学习示例，不是官方范文，也不是用户个人作答。

#### 重点句拆解

| 句子 | 结构与作用 |
| --- | --- |
| Exploration becomes valuable when it is connected with reflection. | 用`when`限定探索发挥价值的条件，避免单纯赞美“多尝试”。 |
| An unsuccessful attempt is also useful because it can reveal what kind of work does not match one's strengths or values. | 把失败尝试转化为信息，`what kind of work`为宾语从句。 |

#### 可迁移句型

| 句型骨架 | 中文作用 | 使用限制 | 替换例句及中文 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ... offers..., allowing somebody to... | 说明资源及其作用 | `allowing`的逻辑主语应与前句事物相符 | Volunteering offers real contact with communities, allowing students to understand social needs. 志愿服务提供与社区真实接触的机会，使学生理解社会需求。 |
| ... becomes valuable when it is connected with... | 限定某事有效的条件 | `be connected with`后接名词 | Practice becomes valuable when it is connected with feedback. 练习与反馈结合时才更有价值。 |
| ... may change as people grow, but... | 承认变化并给出稳定判断标准 | `as`在此表示“随着” | Career plans may change as people grow, but informed choices remain important. 职业规划可能随成长而变化，但知情选择始终重要。 |

#### 主题词汇与搭配

| 表达 | 词性及含义 | 常用搭配 | 易错点 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| explore possibilities | 动词短语；探索可能性 | explore various possibilities | `explore`后直接接宾语 |
| test an interest | 动词短语；通过实践检验兴趣 | test one's interest in a field | 不表示考试意义的“测试” |
| be connected with reflection | 与反思相结合 | connect experience with reflection | 注意`reflection`的双写词形 |
| match one's strengths | 与某人的优势匹配 | match personal strengths and values | `match`可直接接宾语 |
| informed choice | 名词短语；基于充分信息的选择 | make an informed choice | `informed`不是“被通知的选择” |

#### 迁移练习

1. 实践经历能够帮助学生判断一种兴趣是否适合长期发展。
2. 探索只有与反思和反馈结合时，才能产生清晰方向。

<details><summary>参考表达</summary>

1. Practical experience can help students decide whether an interest is suitable for long-term development.
2. Exploration can produce a clear direction only when it is combined with reflection and feedback.

</details>

### 011｜2025年6月第1套｜AI技术与人类创造力

**材料类型：**英语六级写作　　**整理日期：**2026-11-06　　**真题信息：**2025年6月大学英语六级考试写作（第1套）

#### 真题信息与题目要求

> **Directions:** For this part, you are allowed 30 minutes to write an essay that begins with the sentence “With the increasing application of AI technology, there is a growing concern that it may negatively impact human creativity.” You can make comments, cite examples or use your personal experiences to develop your essay. You should write at least 150 words but no more than 200 words. You should copy the sentence given in quotes at the beginning of your essay.

**中文理解：**讨论AI应用增加是否会损害人类创造力，并形成有条件、有措施的观点。指定句必须置于开头；作答时间30分钟，全文150—200词。

#### 审题与写作逻辑

| 段落 | 推进任务 | 避免的问题 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第一段 | 承认担忧并提出“影响取决于使用方式”的立场 | 极端断言AI一定摧毁或一定提升创造力 |
| 第二段 | 分析依赖现成输出与辅助构思两种可能 | 只列AI功能，不解释创造过程 |
| 第三段 | 提出先独立思考、核验和二次创作等方法 | 把AI输出直接等同于个人创造 |

#### 原创学习参考范文

With the increasing application of AI technology, there is a growing concern that it may negatively impact human creativity. This concern is reasonable when people accept machine-generated answers without questioning or improving them. However, the effect of AI depends largely on how it is used.

If students ask an AI tool to complete every task, they may lose opportunities to struggle with problems, connect ideas, and develop an individual voice. Creativity grows through such effort. Yet AI can also support creative work by offering alternative perspectives, organizing information, or helping users test an early idea. A designer, for example, may compare several suggestions and then create a solution based on personal judgment and real needs.

Therefore, people should treat AI as an assistant rather than a substitute for thought. Before using it, students can form their own questions and initial opinions. Afterwards, they should verify the output, identify weak reasoning, and transform useful information into something original. Used in this way, AI does not remove human creativity; it can provide material that thoughtful users examine, challenge, and reshape.

**范文词数：**178词。**说明：**本文为Codex原创学习示例，不是官方范文，也不是用户个人作答。

#### 重点句拆解

| 句子 | 结构与作用 |
| --- | --- |
| However, the effect of AI depends largely on how it is used. | 用`depend on + how从句`把争论转向使用条件。 |
| AI can provide material that thoughtful users examine, challenge, and reshape. | 三个并列动词具体说明人类如何保持主动。 |

#### 可迁移句型

| 句型骨架 | 中文作用 | 使用限制 | 替换例句及中文 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| This concern is reasonable when..., but the effect depends on... | 承认担忧后限定条件 | 不能在没有解释时生硬套用 | This concern is reasonable when data are misused, but the effect depends on the safeguards in place. 数据被滥用时这种担忧合理，但影响取决于现有保障。 |
| ... can support... by doing A, B, or C. | 列举工具的辅助方式 | `by`后使用并列动名词 | Digital tools can support learning by organizing notes, tracking progress, or providing feedback. 数字工具可通过整理笔记、记录进度或提供反馈来辅助学习。 |
| Treat A as B rather than C. | 界定恰当角色 | `as`和`rather than`后的成分保持对应 | Treat feedback as a guide rather than a final judgment. 把反馈当作指南而非最终判决。 |

#### 主题词汇与搭配

| 表达 | 词性及含义 | 常用搭配 | 易错点 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| machine-generated answer | 名词短语；机器生成的答案 | accept a machine-generated answer | 连字符连接复合形容词 |
| individual voice | 名词短语；个人表达风格 | develop an individual voice | 不等同于生理“声音” |
| alternative perspective | 名词短语；另一种视角 | offer alternative perspectives | `alternative`作形容词 |
| personal judgment | 名词短语；个人判断 | rely on personal judgment | `judgment/judgement`两种拼法均可，全文保持一致 |
| substitute for thought | 名词短语；思考的替代物 | become a substitute for thought | `substitute for`固定搭配 |

#### 迁移练习

1. AI可以提供不同视角，但最终判断仍应由使用者作出。
2. 学生应先形成自己的观点，再比较机器生成的答案。

<details><summary>参考表达</summary>

1. AI can offer alternative perspectives, but users should still make the final judgment.
2. Students should form their own views before comparing them with machine-generated answers.

</details>

### 012｜2025年6月第2套｜负责任地使用社交媒体

**材料类型：**英语六级写作　　**整理日期：**2026-11-06　　**真题信息：**2025年6月大学英语六级考试写作（第2套）

#### 真题信息与题目要求

> **Directions:** For this part, you are allowed 30 minutes to write an essay that begins with the sentence “As social media is used more and more extensively, there is a growing awareness of the importance of using it properly and responsibly.” You can make comments, cite examples or use your personal experiences to develop your essay. You should write at least 150 words but no more than 200 words. You should copy the sentence given in quotes at the beginning of your essay.

**中文理解：**论述如何正确、负责任地使用社交媒体。指定句必须置于开头；作答时间30分钟，全文150—200词。

#### 审题与写作逻辑

| 段落 | 推进任务 | 避免的问题 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第一段 | 说明社交媒体的便利与责任问题并存 | 只批评平台，不回应“如何负责使用” |
| 第二段 | 从核验信息、尊重隐私和文明表达展开 | 罗列风险但不给行动标准 |
| 第三段 | 增加时间管理和平台规则，形成个人与平台共同责任 | 把“少用”当作唯一答案 |

#### 原创学习参考范文

As social media is used more and more extensively, there is a growing awareness of the importance of using it properly and responsibly. These platforms make communication convenient and give ordinary people opportunities to share knowledge. At the same time, one careless post can spread false information, invade privacy, or cause unnecessary harm.

Responsible use begins before a user clicks the share button. Information should be checked against reliable sources, especially when it concerns health, safety, or public events. Users should also ask permission before posting another person's photo or private experience. When disagreements arise, criticizing an idea is more constructive than insulting the person who expresses it. Such habits protect both individuals and the quality of online discussion.

Time management is another part of responsible use. Turning off unnecessary notifications and setting limits on recreational browsing can prevent social media from disrupting study and sleep. Platforms should support these efforts through clear rules and effective reporting tools. Social media is neither entirely harmful nor automatically beneficial. Its value depends on informed choices, respect for others, and control over our own attention.

**范文词数：**182词。**说明：**本文为Codex原创学习示例，不是官方范文，也不是用户个人作答。

#### 重点句拆解

| 句子 | 结构与作用 |
| --- | --- |
| Responsible use begins before a user clicks the share button. | 用具体动作引出核验责任，比空泛说“谨慎使用”更可操作。 |
| Criticizing an idea is more constructive than insulting the person who expresses it. | 用比较结构区分对观点的批评与对人的攻击。 |

#### 可迁移句型

| 句型骨架 | 中文作用 | 使用限制 | 替换例句及中文 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| One careless... can..., or... | 提醒一个行为可能产生多重后果 | `can`表示可能性，不夸大为必然 | One careless decision can waste resources or put others at risk. 一个草率决定可能浪费资源或使他人面临风险。 |
| ... should be checked against reliable sources, especially when... | 提出信息核验标准 | `check against`表示与来源比对 | Medical claims should be checked against reliable sources, especially when they affect treatment choices. 医疗说法应与可靠来源核对，尤其当其影响治疗选择时。 |
| ... is neither entirely harmful nor automatically beneficial. | 避免非黑即白的判断 | `neither...nor...`连接同类成分 | Technology is neither entirely harmful nor automatically beneficial. 技术既非完全有害，也不会自动有益。 |

#### 主题词汇与搭配

| 表达 | 词性及含义 | 常用搭配 | 易错点 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| spread false information | 动词短语；传播虚假信息 | prevent the spread of false information | `information`不可数 |
| invade privacy | 动词短语；侵犯隐私 | protect privacy / invade privacy | 不写`invade one's private` |
| reliable source | 名词短语；可靠来源 | check reliable sources | `source`可数，注意单复数 |
| constructive criticism | 名词短语；建设性批评 | offer constructive criticism | `constructive`修饰批评方式 |
| recreational browsing | 名词短语；娱乐性浏览 | limit recreational browsing | `recreational`不同于`recreation`词性 |

#### 迁移练习

1. 转发公共事件信息前，应与多个可靠来源进行核对。
2. 负责任的表达应批评观点，而不是侮辱表达观点的人。

<details><summary>参考表达</summary>

1. Before sharing information about a public event, users should check it against several reliable sources.
2. Responsible expression should criticize an idea rather than insult the person who expresses it.

</details>

<a id="material-013"></a>
### 013｜2025年6月第3套｜大学生的职业准备

**材料类型：**英语六级写作　　**整理日期：**2026-11-06　　**真题信息：**2025年6月大学英语六级考试写作（第3套）

#### 真题信息与题目要求

> **Directions:** For this part, you are allowed 30 minutes to write an essay that begins with the sentence “As requirements for job applications are getting increasingly higher, college students ought to be better prepared for their future career.” You can make comments, cite examples or use your personal experiences to develop your essay. You should write at least 150 words but no more than 200 words. You should copy the sentence given in quotes at the beginning of your essay.

**中文理解：**论述在求职要求提高的背景下，大学生应如何为未来职业做好准备。指定句必须置于开头；作答时间30分钟，全文150—200词。

#### 审题与写作逻辑

| 段落 | 推进任务 | 避免的问题 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第一段 | 解释“更充分准备”包含专业能力与可证明成果 | 把职业准备缩减为制作简历 |
| 第二段 | 从课程基础、项目实践和沟通协作展开 | 堆砌技能名词，不说明如何获得证据 |
| 第三段 | 提出研究岗位、获取反馈和持续更新计划 | 盲目追逐所有热门技能 |

#### 原创学习参考范文

As requirements for job applications are getting increasingly higher, college students ought to be better prepared for their future career. Preparation involves more than collecting certificates or polishing a résumé. Students need solid knowledge, practical evidence of their abilities, and the capacity to learn when job requirements change.

First, they should understand the foundations of their chosen field instead of memorizing isolated tools. Project work can then turn knowledge into visible results. For example, a student interested in data analysis may clean a real dataset, explain the methods used, and evaluate the reliability of the conclusion. Team projects, internships, and volunteer work can also develop communication, responsibility, and an understanding of workplace expectations.

Career preparation should be focused rather than endless. Students can study several target positions, identify the abilities they share, and choose projects that close important gaps. Feedback from teachers, professionals, and teammates can make this plan more realistic. Since careers develop over time, no graduate can prepare for every future task. What matters is building a strong foundation and demonstrating the ability to keep learning.

**范文词数：**179词。**说明：**本文为Codex原创学习示例，不是官方范文，也不是用户个人作答。

#### 重点句拆解

| 句子 | 结构与作用 |
| --- | --- |
| Preparation involves more than collecting certificates or polishing a résumé. | 用`more than doing`纠正“准备等于证书和简历”的狭义理解。 |
| Career preparation should be focused rather than endless. | 用`rather than`强调有目标地补足能力，不追求无限堆砌。 |

#### 可迁移句型

| 句型骨架 | 中文作用 | 使用限制 | 替换例句及中文 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ... involves more than doing A or doing B. | 扩展对概念的理解 | 并列动名词形式保持一致 | Research involves more than collecting data or running software. 科研不只是收集数据或运行软件。 |
| ... can turn knowledge into visible results. | 说明实践如何证明能力 | `turn A into B`固定结构 | A small project can turn classroom knowledge into visible results. 小项目能够把课堂知识转化为可见成果。 |
| What matters is doing... and demonstrating... | 总结最重要的行动 | `is`后使用并列动名词 | What matters is solving real problems and demonstrating clear reasoning. 重要的是解决真实问题并展示清晰推理。 |

#### 主题词汇与搭配

| 表达 | 词性及含义 | 常用搭配 | 易错点 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| solid foundation | 名词短语；扎实基础 | build a solid foundation | `foundation`可数，常用单数 |
| practical evidence | 名词短语；实践证据 | provide practical evidence of ability | `evidence`通常不可数 |
| workplace expectation | 名词短语；职场要求 | understand workplace expectations | 多项要求时用复数 |
| target position | 名词短语；目标岗位 | study target positions | `position`比泛泛的`job`更具体 |
| close a gap | 动词短语；弥补差距 | close a skill gap | 不机械译作“关闭差距” |

#### 迁移练习

1. 项目经历应把课堂知识转化为可检查的结果。
2. 学生可以比较目标岗位，并优先弥补共同的能力缺口。

<details><summary>参考表达</summary>

1. Project experience should turn classroom knowledge into results that can be examined.
2. Students can compare target positions and give priority to the skill gaps they share.

</details>

### 014｜2025年12月第1套｜中国梦与青年自我价值

**材料类型：**英语六级写作　　**整理日期：**2026-11-06　　**真题信息：**2025年12月大学英语六级考试写作（第1套）

#### 真题信息与题目要求

> **Directions:** For this part, you are allowed 30 minutes to write an essay that begins with the sentence “While striving for the Chinese Dream, young people enjoy more opportunities to realize their self-worth.” You can make comments, cite examples or use your personal experiences to develop your essay. You should write at least 150 words but no more than 200 words. You should copy the sentence given in quotes at the beginning of your essay.

**中文理解：**论述青年在为中国梦奋斗的过程中，如何把社会发展机遇转化为个人价值。指定句必须置于开头；作答时间30分钟，全文150—200词。

#### 审题与写作逻辑

| 段落 | 推进任务 | 避免的问题 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第一段 | 把国家发展与青年成长建立具体联系 | 只重复宏大口号，不说明机会是什么 |
| 第二段 | 从学习、创新、基层服务或专业实践举例 | 把自我价值等同于个人收入和名望 |
| 第三段 | 强调能力、责任与长期行动 | 忽视个人选择和实际贡献 |

#### 原创学习参考范文

While striving for the Chinese Dream, young people enjoy more opportunities to realize their self-worth. Social development creates new fields of study, work, and public service, but an opportunity becomes meaningful only when young people prepare themselves to use it responsibly.

Self-worth can be realized through both personal growth and contribution to others. A student may acquire professional knowledge and use it to improve a medical service, develop a useful technology, or solve a problem in a local community. Such work may begin with an ordinary task, yet it becomes valuable when it answers a real need. In the process, young people gain skills, confidence, and a clearer understanding of their social responsibility.

To make good use of present opportunities, young people should continue learning, pay attention to practical problems, and remain willing to cooperate with people from different backgrounds. They also need patience, because meaningful contribution is rarely achieved immediately. When personal goals are connected with the needs of society, individual progress and national development can reinforce each other. This connection gives self-worth a solid foundation in action rather than in empty claims.

**范文词数：**184词。**说明：**本文为Codex原创学习示例，不是官方范文，也不是用户个人作答。

#### 重点句拆解

| 句子 | 结构与作用 |
| --- | --- |
| An opportunity becomes meaningful only when young people prepare themselves to use it responsibly. | `only when`限定机会转化为价值的条件。 |
| When personal goals are connected with the needs of society, individual progress and national development can reinforce each other. | 把个人与社会的关系写成相互促进，避免单向口号。 |

#### 可迁移句型

| 句型骨架 | 中文作用 | 使用限制 | 替换例句及中文 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ... becomes meaningful only when... | 强调实现价值的条件 | `only when`位于从句中不倒装 | Technology becomes meaningful only when it solves real problems. 技术只有在解决真实问题时才有意义。 |
| ... can be realized through both A and B. | 从两个方面解释价值实现 | `both...and...`连接同类成分 | Personal growth can be realized through both learning and service. 个人成长可以通过学习和服务共同实现。 |
| When A is connected with B, C and D can reinforce each other. | 总结两个目标相互促进 | 注意单复数和被动语态 | When education is connected with practice, knowledge and ability can reinforce each other. 教育与实践结合时，知识和能力能够相互促进。 |

#### 主题词汇与搭配

| 表达 | 词性及含义 | 常用搭配 | 易错点 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| realize one's self-worth | 动词短语；实现自我价值 | opportunities to realize self-worth | `realize`在此不是“意识到” |
| social development | 名词短语；社会发展 | contribute to social development | `development`通常不可数 |
| public service | 名词短语；公共服务 | work in public service | 依语境可数或不可数 |
| answer a real need | 动词短语；回应真实需求 | design a service that answers a real need | 比`meet a need`更强调回应，二者均可 |
| social responsibility | 名词短语；社会责任 | develop a sense of social responsibility | 常与`sense of`搭配 |

#### 迁移练习

1. 机会只有与准备和行动结合时，才能转化为个人价值。
2. 当个人目标回应社会需求时，两者能够相互促进。

<details><summary>参考表达</summary>

1. An opportunity can be turned into personal value only when it is combined with preparation and action.
2. When personal goals answer social needs, the two can reinforce each other.

</details>
